text-navy sm:text-2xl">Course introduction</h2></div></div><div class="mt-5 space-y-4 text-[0.95rem] leading-relaxed text-slate-700"><p>The US Mortgage Underwriting program at VertexIT is built for candidates who want to enter the US mortgage and lending operations industry. The curriculum is structured around the real underwriting workflow — from understanding loan types and mortgage lifecycle to credit, income and asset analysis, debt-to-income ratios, LTV/CLTV concepts, automated underwriting and case-study based practice.
